# Written by tools/coverage.py collect from runs of the original (--no-hooks, vanilla) with EI_COVER=<the
# rewrites' addresses>; scenarios last collected before S21c came from the hook build (EI_COVER=hooks).
# ADDRESS NAME BITS: a rewritten function, and a hex bitset over the scenarios below (bit i: scenario i
# reaches it). tools/coverage.py list turns it into coverage.csv.
